## Q98. There has been a persistent deficit budget year after year. Which of the following...
**Primary route:** Economy → [Union Budget, Fiscal Policy and Deficit Indicators](../../topics/economy/union-budget-fiscal-policy-and-deficit-indicators.md) → There has been a persistent deficit budget year after year. Which of the following...  
**Primary classification:** Economy → Union Budget, Fiscal Policy and Deficit Indicators → There has been a persistent deficit budget year after year. Which of the following...  
**Metadata:** 2015 · Prelims · GS-I · Series A · Official Q98 · Difficulty: Moderate · Demand: factual/conceptual statement or option evaluation · elimination  
**Canonical owner:** [Economy/basic/09_Union-Budget-Fiscal-Policy-and-Deficit-Indicators.md](../../../upsc-ai-kit/knowledge/Economy/basic/09_Union-Budget-Fiscal-Policy-and-Deficit-Indicators.md)  
**Canonical slug:** `economy-there-has-been-a-persistent-deficit-budget-year-after-year-which-of-the-following`  
**Backlink:** [Paper-wise index](../../catalogue/PAPERWISE-INDEX.md)

### Official Series-A question (scan-reconciled transcription)

There has been a persistent deficit budget year after year. Which of the following actions can be taken by the government to reduce the deficit?  
1. Reducing revenue expenditure  
2. Introducing new welfare schemes  
3. Rationalizing subsidies  
4. Expanding industries  
Select the correct answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 and 3 only  
(b) 2 and 3 only  
(c) 1 only  
(d) 1, 2, 3 and 4  

**Answer (UPSC official-final Series-A key): (A) — 1 and 3 only**

### Statement/option evaluation, elimination logic and trap

A deficit budget occurs when the government's total expenditure exceeds its total revenue. To reduce this deficit, the government needs to either increase its revenue or decrease its expenditure. Let's analyze the given actions: 1. Reducing revenue expenditure: Revenue expenditure includes spending on salaries, subsidies, interest payments, etc. Directly reducing these expenses would lower the government's total expenditure and thus help reduce the deficit. This statement is correct. 2. Introducing new welfare schemes: New welfare schemes typically involve increased government spending, which would exacerbate (increase) the deficit, not reduce it. This statement is incorrect. 3. Rationalizing subsidies: Subsidies are a significant component of government expenditure. Rationalizing them (e.g., reducing the amount, targeting them better, or eliminating non-essential ones) would directly decrease government spending and help reduce the deficit. This statement is correct. 4. Expanding industries: While expanding industries can potentially lead to higher tax revenues in the long run, it often requires initial capital outlay or support from the government, which could increase expenditure in the short to medium term. As a direct and immediate measure to *reduce* the *current* deficit, this is not the most effective action. This statement is incorrect as a primary deficit-reduction measure. Therefore, reducing revenue expenditure and rationalizing subsidies are effective actions to reduce a budget deficit.

**Statement/item-level result:**
- **Official keyed answer:** (A) — 1 and 3 only. The propositions, institutional associations, chronology or factual pairs are resolved by the specific reasoning above; every competing code fails wherever it includes a rejected item or omits a required one.

**Option-by-option check:**
- **A: 1 and 3 only** — Correct: it matches the official-final Series-A key and the controlling facts explained above.
- **B: 2 and 3 only** — Incorrect: it conflicts with the controlling facts above or, for a statement-code item, includes an incorrect proposition/omits a correct proposition relative to option A.
- **C: 1 only** — Incorrect: it conflicts with the controlling facts above or, for a statement-code item, includes an incorrect proposition/omits a correct proposition relative to option A.
- **D: 1, 2, 3 and 4** — Incorrect: it conflicts with the controlling facts above or, for a statement-code item, includes an incorrect proposition/omits a correct proposition relative to option A.

**Elimination logic:** Identify the decisive fact in **there has been a persistent deficit budget year after year. which of the following...**, test each statement, pair or institutional association independently, and remove every option contradicted by that fact. Treat elimination as corroboration, not proof.  
**UPSC trap:** A plausible near-association, wrong institution/location, absolute qualifier, reversed chronology, or partly correct statement is embedded in a competing option.

## Q99. Has/have been accorded 'Geographical Indication' status?
**Primary route:** Science and Technology → [Intellectual Property Rights and Patents](../../topics/science-and-technology/intellectual-property-rights-and-patents.md) → Has/have been accorded 'Geographical Indication' status?

**Canonical owner:** [Science-and-Technology/basic/17_Intellectual-Property-Rights-and-Patents.md](../../../upsc-ai-kit/knowledge/Science-and-Technology/basic/17_Intellectual-Property-Rights-and-Patents.md)  
**Canonical slug:** `science-and-technology-has-have-been-accorded-geographical-indication-status`  
**Backlink:** [Paper-wise index](../../catalogue/PAPERWISE-INDEX.md)

### Official Series-A question (scan-reconciled transcription)

Which of the following has/have been accorded 'Geographical Indication' status?  
1. Banaras Brocades and Sarees  
2. Rajasthani Daal-Bati-Churma  
3. Tirupathi Laddu  
Select the correct answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 only  
(b) 2 and 3 only  
(c) 1 and 3 only  
(d) 1, 2 and 3  

**Answer (UPSC official-final Series-A key): (C) — 1 and 3 only**

### Statement/option evaluation, elimination logic and trap

A Geographical Indication (GI) tag is a sign used on products that have a specific geographical origin and possess qualities or a reputation that are due to that origin. Let's check the status of the given items: 1. Banaras Brocades and Sarees: Yes, Banaras Brocades and Sarees (Varanasi Brocades and Sarees) from Uttar Pradesh have been granted the Geographical Indication status. This recognizes their unique weaving tradition and craftsmanship originating from Varanasi. 2. Rajasthani Daal-Bati-Churma: No, Rajasthani Daal-Bati-Churma, a popular traditional dish, has not been accorded GI status. GI tags are primarily for agricultural, natural, manufactured goods, or handicrafts, not typically for prepared food dishes in this manner. 3. Tirupathi Laddu: Yes, the famous Tirupathi Laddu, the prasadam offered at the Tirumala Venkateswara Temple in Tirupati, Andhra Pradesh, has been accorded GI status. This protects its unique identity and the traditional method of its preparation. Therefore, only Banaras Brocades and Sarees and Tirupathi Laddu have received GI status among the given options.

**Statement/item-level result:**
- **Official keyed answer:** (C) — 1 and 3 only. The propositions, institutional associations, chronology or factual pairs are resolved by the specific reasoning above; every competing code fails wherever it includes a rejected item or omits a required one.

**Option-by-option check:**
- **A: 1 only** — Incorrect: it conflicts with the controlling facts above or, for a statement-code item, includes an incorrect proposition/omits a correct proposition relative to option C.
- **B: 2 and 3 only** — Incorrect: it conflicts with the controlling facts above or, for a statement-code item, includes an incorrect proposition/omits a correct proposition relative to option C.
- **C: 1 and 3 only** — Correct: it matches the official-final Series-A key and the controlling facts explained above.
- **D: 1, 2 and 3** — Incorrect: it conflicts with the controlling facts above or, for a statement-code item, includes an incorrect proposition/omits a correct proposition relative to option C.

**Elimination logic:** Identify the decisive fact in **has/have been accorded 'geographical indication' status?**, test each statement, pair or institutional association independently, and remove every option contradicted by that fact. Treat elimination as corroboration, not proof.  
**UPSC trap:** A plausible near-association, wrong institution/location, absolute qualifier, reversed chronology, or partly correct statement is embedded in a competing option.

### Official Series-A question (scan-reconciled transcription)

With reference to the Indian Renewable Energy Development Agency Limited (IREDA), which of the following statements is/are correct?  
1. It is a Public Limited Government Company.  
2. It is a Non-Banking Financial Company.  
Select the correct answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 only  
(b) 2 only  
(c) Both 1 and 2  
(d) Neither 1 nor 2  

**Answer (UPSC official-final Series-A key): (C) — Both 1 and 2**

### Statement/option evaluation, elimination logic and trap

Let's examine the statements about the Indian Renewable Energy Development Agency Limited (IREDA): 1. It is a Public Limited Government Company: This statement is correct. IREDA is a Mini Ratna (Category-I) company under the administrative control of the Ministry of New and Renewable Energy (MNRE). It is registered as a Public Limited Company under the Companies Act, 1956. 2. It is a Non-Banking Financial Company: This statement is correct. IREDA is registered with the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) as a Non-Banking Financial Company (NBFC). Its primary role is to provide financial assistance for renewable energy and energy efficiency/conservation projects in India. Since both statements are correct, the answer is both 1 and 2.
